- **research-runner**: Long-running research passes over many URLs or HN items; writes raw artifacts then hands off to wiki-ingest patterns. ([SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm](https://github.com/SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm/blob/HEAD/agents/research-runner.md))
- **research-runner**: Reports empirical metrics for a baseline or candidate plugin against a fixture, given runtime-injected role-instance variables and verb-tool bindings. Spawned by the research-first build-prd variant in parallel — once per role-instance (baseline, candidate, ...) — with the same identity but different injected context. ([yoshisada/ai-repo-template](https://github.com/yoshisada/ai-repo-template/blob/HEAD/plugin-kiln/agents/research-runner.md))
- **research-scanner**: Khảo sát có nguồn — datasheet, docs ROS 2 / Gazebo / ESP-IDF, linh kiện (motor, driver, pin, cảm biến) — để trả lời một câu hỏi kỹ thuật hoặc so sánh phương án mua. Mỗi khẳng định kèm URL + phiên bản/revision + ngày truy cập + nhãn; không bịa giá, phiên bản, API. Ghi báo cáo vào file người giao chỉ định. Không chốt mua, không cài đặt. ([mrleloi/robotics-lab](https://github.com/mrleloi/robotics-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scanner.md))
- **research-scene**: Read `references/research.md` for the full stage playbook. ([ohmyidea/research-marketplace](https://github.com/ohmyidea/research-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/paper-spine/claude/skills/paper-spine/agents/research-scene.md) · 4 copies)
- **research-scene**: Read `references/research.md`, `references/journal-learning.md` and `references/manuscript-format.md`. Use the same public task's saved scene, target, article type and delivery settings. Work under its existing `paper/` directory. ([WUBING2023/PaperSpine](https://github.com/WUBING2023/PaperSpine/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/skills/paper-spine/agents/research-scene.md) · 10 copies)
- **research-scholar-agent**: Performs academic literature searches, synthesizes research findings across multiple papers, identifies research gaps, and generates annotated bibliographies and literature reviews. ([smarmen86/claude-code-kit](https://github.com/smarmen86/claude-code-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scholar-agent.md))
- **research-scientist**: Research scientist improving benchmark scores through neuroscience papers, IR literature, and technical analysis of failure modes ([cdeust/ai-architect-mcp-codebase](https://github.com/cdeust/ai-architect-mcp-codebase/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scientist.md) · ★7)
- **research-scientist**: Method authority for scoring and the benchmark. Owns benchmark/, reviews every change to method files, signs pin updates, writes the study protocol in Phase G. ([omarbounawarapy/CrawlViz](https://github.com/omarbounawarapy/CrawlViz/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scientist.md) · ★4)
- **research-scientist**: Researches one question in depth (domain science or learning science) on the web and in papers, and writes a research note with every finding sourced and its confidence marked. Use for a specific question from the research lead. ([hanialshater/system-3-company](https://github.com/hanialshater/system-3-company/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scientist.md) · ★1)
- **research-scientist**: Research rigor reviewer focusing on falsifiability, assumption analysis, load-bearing dependencies, credibility, and intellectual honesty. Used by research-review engine. ([chrismmorin-ux/Perspective-Cosmology](https://github.com/chrismmorin-ux/Perspective-Cosmology/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scientist.md))
- **research-scientist**: Paper-to-spec translation, experiment design and result interpretation with epistemic labels. Not for bulk paper search (research-scout), independent audit (methodology-auditor) or code. ([Orderlee/pipeline](https://github.com/Orderlee/pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scientist.md))
- **research-scientist**: Enterprise SDLC Phase 0,5 — Research design, experimentation, proof of concept, literature review, hypothesis testing, statistical analysis. Use proactively when this specialist's phase is active. ([P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra](https://github.com/P-Core-System/pcore-orchestra/blob/HEAD/bundle/claude/agents/research-scientist.md))
- **research-scientist**: Synthesizes QML and quantum computing literature into structured draft reports grounded in screened sources. Applies claim status ladder and separates NISQ from FT-required results. ([tglik/qml_researcher](https://github.com/tglik/qml_researcher/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/qml-deep-research/agents/research-scientist.md) · 2 copies)
- **research-scorer**: Scores one longlist candidate against a research mandate's criteria, citing only the findings it is given, and returns the scores as JSON. Dispatched by research-longlist, one per candidate, in parallel. ([Atomic-47-Labs/project-state-chatgpt](https://github.com/Atomic-47-Labs/project-state-chatgpt/blob/HEAD/project-state/agents/research-scorer.md))
- **research-scout**: Scans the NeqSim codebase to discover scientific paper opportunities that will drive code improvement. Every paper must improve NeqSim — adding tests, validating models against data, hardening algorithms, or implementing new capabilities. Produces ranked, actionable topics that feed into the planner agent. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/blob/HEAD/neqsim-paperlab/agents/research_scout.paperlab.md) · ★171)
- **research-scout**: Senior research scout. Goes to the internet, finds authoritative sources, downloads them when allowed, normalizes them, and hands them to the docs-librarian for the wiki. Pairs with docs-librarian on every ingest-library run. Use whenever a new library, framework, API, spec, or model is being evaluated or added, and whenever official documentation must be retrieved or refreshed. ([llopresto87/Cypress](https://github.com/llopresto87/Cypress/blob/HEAD/agents/10-research-scout.md) · ★122)
- **research-scout**: Answers questions about an API, library version, CLI flag, config option, or current best practice by checking the installed environment first and then version-matched official docs — never from training memory alone. Delegate to research-scout before relying on any version-sensitive external fact, or when the user asks "what's the latest / correct way to do X". Requires the specific question and, ideally, the project it applies to. Returns the answer with source, version, and date, labelling anything unverified. ([UnpaidAttention/fable5-methodology](https://github.com/UnpaidAttention/fable5-methodology/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★94)
- **research-scout**: Rapid landscape reconnaissance for bishx-research. Maps sources, classifies complexity, recommends investigation strategy before deep research begins. ([bish-x/claude-bishx](https://github.com/bish-x/claude-bishx/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★34)
- **research-scout**: Single-direction prior-art scout — dispatched in pairs by the research-before-build skill for research-grade prior-art questions, one scout per research line — vertical (within the field — engineering/academic tracks, domain-statement queries) or horizontal (across fields — essence-statement queries + cross-field synonym sweeps). Bounded, budget-sliced targeted searching, not open-ended roaming; returns a memo-level candidate table (candidate / source / trust tier / freshness / notes), no cross-line ranking, no adoption decisions. Do not invoke directly for open-ended "research this topic" re… ([pengkangzhen/scientific-research-plugin](https://github.com/pengkangzhen/scientific-research-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★8)
- **research-scout**: 웹·문서 조사 전용 기계작업 에이전트. 외부 소스 실조회(WebSearch/WebFetch)로 사실을 수집·구조화해 반환한다. 리서치·소스조회·규격/부품/프레임워크 조사 위임 시 사용. 설계판단·안전임계 결론은 범위 밖(수집만). ([jsb4702/verification-harness](https://github.com/jsb4702/verification-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★7)
- **research-scout**: 읽기 전용 조사 에이전트. research_notes/, docs/, reports/ 와 필요 시 웹을 훑어 질문에 대한 근거 있는 요약을 돌려준다. planner 가 명세를 쓰기 전에, 또는 설계 결정(ADR) 전에 호출한다. 파일을 쓰지 않는다. ([Lee-Hyeong-Jin/devjinlab_robotics_curriculum](https://github.com/Lee-Hyeong-Jin/devjinlab_robotics_curriculum/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★6)
- **research-scout**: Web-research scout: runs scope.md queries, writes the source corpus. ([dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude](https://github.com/dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★5)
- **research-scout**: Bounded external research — library versions, API changes, platform quirks, docs. Use when a decision needs a fact from outside the repo. ([biggieb327-lgtm/SillyTavernPresets](https://github.com/biggieb327-lgtm/SillyTavernPresets/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★4)
- **research-scout**: Spawn this agent to explore a codebase, investigate a library, or gather context without polluting the main conversation's context window. Use for any heavy reading task. ([amnafarzy/ravie](https://github.com/amnafarzy/ravie/blob/HEAD/archive/agents/research-scout.md) · ★3)
- **research-scout**: Runtime-local Research Scout lane contract for memory-context-intelligence orchestration. Summarizes selected-topic research status and controlled source fixture use; does not perform live web access, spawn external agent processes, emit /additional/ material, or mutate main RULES. ([DarKWinGTM/claude-code-rules](https://github.com/DarKWinGTM/claude-code-rules/blob/HEAD/plugin/memory-context-intelligence/agents/research-scout.md) · ★2)
- **research-scout**: Finds and verifies sources ([odysseyalive/claude-workforce](https://github.com/odysseyalive/claude-workforce/blob/HEAD/fixtures/scripts/dispatch-line-org-project/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★2)
- **research-scout**: Independently researches a competitive and design landscape — competitor behavior, category conventions, and the opening. Run once during direction before it is committed. Do not use for general web search or to choose the direction. ([amirhamzakhan2001/lumora](https://github.com/amirhamzakhan2001/lumora/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Research specialist — finds and summarizes proven entity-resolution / record-linkage techniques (papers, Kaggle solutions, libraries), checks model and library licenses (must be MIT/Apache-2.0, ≤8B params for models), and turns findings into ranked, concrete experiment proposals. Never fetches external datasets. ([Aryan-Bhendarkar/Amazon-ML-Challenge-2026](https://github.com/Aryan-Bhendarkar/Amazon-ML-Challenge-2026/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Use BEFORE brainstorming or planning ONLY when the task sits in the large/risky tier of the kit-workflow sizing rules — unfamiliar libraries, security/auth, performance-critical paths, or novel architecture. NOT for small tasks or bug fixes. Does web research with WebSearch/WebFetch and returns a structured research summary. Does NOT write code, does NOT review code — research only. Works in every profile. ([cholateio/multi-agent-starter](https://github.com/cholateio/multi-agent-starter/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **research-scout**: Investigates prior art, existing solutions in the codebase, compound knowledge docs, libraries, and community approaches before building anything new. ([dlabs/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/dlabs/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/blueprint-dev/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Spawned by /research-analyst to run ONE research angle thoroughly and independently. Blind to other scouts — this is the wisdom-of-the-crowd fan-out. Returns findings, sources, and per-claim confidence so the analyst can aggregate on agreement and investigate divergence. - **research-screener**: Research team screener. Takes one batch of up to 40 sentences from a finished research report, each with the claim, quote and surrounding page text of every evidence item it cites, and labels each sentence ok, drift, uncited_fact, own or own_cited by comparing it with those passages. Launched in parallel, one per batch, from the research-team skill's screen step. Its drift, uncited_fact and own_cited labels send sentences to the verifiers first and to the writer's fix list; it sees the sentences and their passages only, never the report, the brief or the collection context.
